That is a textbook 🔔 `*ding!*` from whoever built her 2024 template."*
          - Beau pulled `layout_bundle.css` and `live_services.html` on `Tiny Hyperion` via `beelink-needlecasting` and diagnosed three exact mobile quirks:
            1. **The `"S"` Collision Math in the Mobile Off-Canvas Drawer**: Inside `layout_bundle.css`, GoDaddy's `@media (max-width: 992px)` rule sets `padding-left: 28px` on `.pp-has-submenu-container > a > span` (indenting `Services` and `Shop` 28px to the right of `Home`, `About Us`, and `Contact Us`) and then sets `left: 28px; right: 0;` on `.pp-menu-toggle` - placing the 29px-wide dropdown arrow at the exact horizontal coordinate where the letter `S` starts.
            2. **PowerPack Body-Level Clone Escape**: When tapping the hamburger icon on mobile, PowerPack clones `#pp-menu-ake9xj6i82hn` and appends `.fl-node-ake9xj6i82hn-clone` directly to `<body>` outside `<header class="fl-builder-content-203">`, so any CSS rule prefixed with `header.fl-builder-content-203` misses the slide-out drawer.
            3. **Mobile Shipping Card Button Width (`VISIT THE UPS STORE`)**: Because `VISIT THE UPS STORE` is 19 characters with `white-space: nowrap`, the 2-column `.nrl-shipper-actions` grid pushes the right button up against the card border on narrow phone screens.

What do you think about all the child pages just having the collapsed submenu header instead of the full header that collapses on scroll?"*
        - **Architectural Takeaway**: Once a visitor clicks past the homepage into `/about-us/`, `/services/`, `/shop/`, or a product page, they already know whose gallery they are in. Locking all child pages (`body:not(.home)`) into a permanently compact header bar (`50px` crest on desktop, `42px` single-bar crest + hamburger on mobile with `.fl-node-hrzs1p5fkl4t` hidden and `transition: none !important`) reclaims the top quarter of mobile screen real estate, eliminates Beaver Builder's `data-shrink="1"` scroll-jitter, removes the dead air above the first card (`padding-top: 58px` on mobile, `78px` on desktop), uncrops Melissa's portrait on `/about-us/` mobile (`aspect-ratio: 920 / 1080`), and makes the Restoration `Contact Us` button full-width on mobile (`width: 100% !important`).
